This neuron is mostly a suppression unit: it goes strongly negative on formulaic web-boilerplate tokens ("the" in "the drop down above", "RSS"/"2.0" in "RSS feed", clause-final periods in generic advice text), while its weak positive firing lands on the modifier word inside compound noun phrases ("side sleepers", "cattle feed", "storytellers", "a fast one") and on list-joining "and"/commas in recipe-style enumerations.
